# Skills · I · part 10

761 entries, ig-human to ijcai-related-work. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ig-human**: Humanize an Instagram caption, Reel script, comment, reply or DM: strip em dashes, AI slop words and invisible characters with humanize.py, then score it with detect.py. Use when the user asks to humanize or de-slop Instagram copy, asks if an Instagram draft sounds like AI, or when another ig-* skill hands over a draft. ([solutionsystempro/instagram-agent-skill](https://github.com/solutionsystempro/instagram-agent-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/ig-human))
- **ig-humanizer**: Remove the AI tells readers react to in an Instagram caption or carousel slide: 2026 vocabulary by density, reveal bridges, staccato stacks, stacked triads, performed sincerity, emoji storms; caps em dashes. Includes --mode audit (first-125 hook, length, hashtags, emoji, CTA, media) and --mode profile. Not for beating AI detectors (no edit reliably does). Not for writing from scratch (use ig-caption-writer or ig-carousel-planner). Keywords: humanize, de-AI caption, audit before posting. ([sergebulaev/instagram-skills](https://github.com/sergebulaev/instagram-skills/tree/HEAD/.codex-marketplace/instagram-skills/skills/ig-humanizer) · ★875 · 15 copies)
- **ig-humanizer**: Remove AI tells from an Instagram caption or carousel text, or audit a caption before posting. Not for beating AI detectors or writing from scratch. ([pedroxcardoso/insta-metrics](https://github.com/pedroxcardoso/insta-metrics/tree/HEAD/skills/ig-humanizer) · ★2)
- **ig-humanizer**: Scrub AI tells from any Instagram caption or carousel slide text, or audit a finished caption against the 2026 checklist. Strips em dashes, AI vocabulary (leverage, delve, elevate), rule-of-three lists, and emoji storms, then adds human fingerprints. Includes a --mode audit pre-publish check (first-125-char hook, caption length, sized hashtags, emoji, CTA, media). Triggers on humanize, de-AI my caption, audit before posting. Not for writing from scratch (use ig-caption-writer or ig-carousel-planner). ([therealtimex/signals](https://github.com/therealtimex/signals/tree/HEAD/docs-dev/refs/instagram-skills/ig-humanizer) · ★1)
- **ig-ingest**: Download an Instagram reel, post, or carousel, analyze its full content, and store the extracted content in the user's Notion "Social Media Posts Link" database via Composio. Use whenever the user runs /ingest or pastes an instagram.com URL asking to save/ingest/analyze it. ([riddhimaaan/instagram-ingest](https://github.com/riddhimaaan/instagram-ingest/tree/HEAD/skills/ig-ingest))
- **ig-insights**: Read the user's Instagram niche and any account from real public data: the reels traveling under a hashtag right now, follower and posting stats for a handle, and each account's median views so a breakout is measured against its own normal. Feeds /ig-viral's swipe file directly. Use when the user says "what's working under this hashtag", "scan the niche", "pull this account's stats", "how big is this competitor", "benchmark me against", or wants /ig-viral's research without collecting it by hand. ([nessalazne/instagram-agent-skill](https://github.com/nessalazne/instagram-agent-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/ig-insights) · ★9)

- **ig-lead-simple**: IG Lead Magnet Simple — v1 simplified loop. Asset creation → publish → comment-keyword trigger → DM Google Drive URL directly. Differs from v2: no Intercom, no email capture, no email sequence, no nurture; DM hands the link straight to the user. Carousel images use VPick `gpt-image-2`. Designed for "fast single-shot run, single-product PRD-driven" scenarios. Triggers on phrases like "ig-lead-simple", "simple IG lead magnet", "no email", "DM the drive link", "fast one-shot run", "follow my product PRD". ([snoopyrain/ig-lead-simple](https://github.com/snoopyrain/ig-lead-simple/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))

- **ig-marketer**: Daily Instagram content worker for any iOS app. Researches the target niche on Instagram, generates carousels and reels, drafts posts for human to publish via Postiz, pulls analytics + RevenueCat conversions daily, and iterates experiments until MRR reaches the target. Use when running the daily marketing loop, generating content, checking analytics, or updating the content strategy. Requires references/config.json to be filled before Cycle 0. All tools and workflows are self-contained in references/. ([iamhenry/programmatic-ugc](https://github.com/iamhenry/programmatic-ugc/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/ig-marketer))
- **ig-network**: Hara Vital network audit on Instagram. Reads active professionals from Supabase (read-only) and, by default, audits only the ones not audited yet; with --all it audits everyone. Opens each Instagram handle in the logged-in Chrome, classifies each one as valid, invalid or renamed_or_uncertain, checks whether @haravital already follows them, and writes a log plus a report. Read-only: it never follows, comments, shares or edits Supabase. Use when the user says "ig-network", "auditar los instagram de los profesionales", "revisar los handles", "chequear qué profesionales seguimos", or asks whether… ([mbelenmontoya/hara](https://github.com/mbelenmontoya/hara/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/ig-network))
